A) Grand and well-maintained

B) Humble and in disrepair

C) Modern and newly built

D) Large and imposing

Answer: B

Q13: How long before Elizabeth-Jane and Farfrae's arrival had Henchard passed away?

A) About an hour

B) About half-an-hour

C) Just a few minutes

D) The night before

Answer: B

Q14: How did Henchard treat Abel Whittle's mother when she was alive?

A) He was indifferent to her

B) He was kind to her

C) He was cruel to her

D) He ignored her existence

Answer: B

Q15: What did Henchard say when Abel Whittle told him he followed because he cared about him?

B) "I don't need your pity."

C) "You're a poor fond fool to care for a wretch like me."

D) "Leave me alone, Whittle."

Answer: C

B) Elizabeth-Jane and Farfrae brought them

C) Some neighbourly woodmen lent them

D) They were already in the cottage

Answer: C

Q17: What did Henchard pin to the head of his bed before he died?

B) A piece of paper with his will

C) A picture of Susan

D) Money for his burial

Answer: B

Q18: Who wrote Henchard's will?

A) Abel Whittle

B) Elizabeth-Jane

C) Donald Farfrae

D) Henchard himself

Answer: D

Q19: What was the first stipulation in Henchard's will?

A) That no man remember him

B) That Elizabeth-Jane not be told of his death

C) That he not be buried in consecrated ground

D) That no flowers be planted on his grave

Answer: B

Q20: What was Henchard's last stipulation in his will?

A) That no man remember him

B) That Elizabeth-Jane not be told of his death

C) That he not be buried in consecrated ground

D) That no flowers be planted on his grave

Answer: A

Q21: Why did Elizabeth-Jane respect Henchard's dying wishes?

C) Because she knew he meant what he said

D) Because Farfrae insisted on it

Answer: C

D) She was indifferent about it

Answer: B

A) Stormy and turbulent

B) Calm and peaceful

C) Gloomy and overcast

D) Humid and oppressive

Answer: B

Q24: How did Elizabeth-Jane's teaching affect her?

B) It made her realize the importance of social status

D) It made her long for wider interests

Answer: C

Q26: What did Elizabeth-Jane wonder about despite her good fortune?

Answer: C

Answer: B

17. What did Henchard pin to the head of his bed before he died?
   A. A letter to Elizabeth-Jane
   B. A piece of paper with his will
   C. A picture of Susan
   D. Money for his burial
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

18. Who wrote Henchard's will?
   A. Abel Whittle
   B. Elizabeth-Jane
   C. Donald Farfrae
   D. Henchard himself
   **उत्तर:** D
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

19. What was the first stipulation in Henchard's will?
   A. That no man remember him
   B. That Elizabeth-Jane not be told of his death
   C. That he not be buried in consecrated ground
   D. That no flowers be planted on his grave
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

20. What was Henchard's last stipulation in his will?
   A. That no man remember him
   B. That Elizabeth-Jane not be told of his death
   C. That he not be buried in consecrated ground
   D. That no flowers be planted on his grave
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

21. Why did Elizabeth-Jane respect Henchard's dying wishes?
   A. Because she feared supernatural consequences if she didn't
   B. Because she wanted to honor him
   C. Because she knew he meant what he said
   D. Because Farfrae insisted on it
   **उत्तर:** C
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

22. How did Elizabeth-Jane feel about her last parting with Henchard?
   A. She was relieved to be rid of him
   B. She regretted her unkindness
   C. She felt it was appropriate
   D. She was indifferent about it
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

23. What kind of weather did Elizabeth-Jane find herself in after Henchard's death?
   A. Stormy and turbulent
   B. Calm and peaceful
   C. Gloomy and overcast
   D. Humid and oppressive
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

24. How did Elizabeth-Jane's teaching affect her?
   A. It made her discontent with her life
   B. It made her realize the importance of social status
   C. It made her think she could be content in any social position
   D. It made her long for wider interests
   **उत्तर:** C
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

25. What did Elizabeth-Jane think about the honor of a brief transit through a sorry world?
   A. It called for great celebration
   B. It hardly called for effusiveness
   C. It was the greatest achievement in life
   D. It should be mourned
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

26. What did Elizabeth-Jane wonder about despite her good fortune?
   A. Why she had been chosen for happiness
   B. The persistence of the unforeseen
   C. How to gain even more in life
   D. What she had done to deserve it
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

27. What did Elizabeth-Jane's youth seem to teach her?
   A. That happiness was the norm in life
   B. That pain was just an occasional episode
   C. That happiness was the occasional episode in a drama of pain
   D. That life was equally balanced between happiness and pain
   **उत्तर:** C
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

28. What did Elizabeth-Jane consider her married life to be compared to her preceding years?
   A. Equally troubling
   B. A great disappointment
   C. A latitude of calm weather
   D. An exciting adventure
   **उत्तर:** C
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

29. How did Elizabeth-Jane feel about her position in life?
   A. She felt it was far beneath what she deserved
   B. She felt it offered much to be thankful for
   C. She felt it was only a temporary state before greater success
   D. She felt it was a punishment for her past actions
   **उत्तर:** B
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।

30. What didn't blind Elizabeth-Jane from the fact that others received less than they deserved?
   A. Her belief that no one deserved less than they got
   B. Her preoccupation with her own happiness
   C. Her desire to be accepted in high society
   D. Her resentment towards those more fortunate
   **उत्तर:** A
   **व्याख्या:** उत्तर स्रोत पुस्तक की उत्तर-कुंजी के अनुसार है।
